820 entries, meeting-synthesis to melonjs-getting-started. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **meeting-synthesis**: Turns meeting transcripts or notes into a durable record of decisions made, actions assigned, risks raised, and questions left open. Separates what was decided from what was discussed, and preserves uncertainty honestly instead of smoothing it into false resolution. For a roofer's brain, this covers adjuster meetings, homeowner walk-throughs, crew briefings, subcontractor negotiations, and Cleverwork strategy sessions. ([Clvrwrk/a-roofer-open-brain](https://github.com/Clvrwrk/a-roofer-open-brain/tree/HEAD/skills/ob1/meeting-synthesis))

- **meeting-synthesize**: Cross-meeting archaeology skill. Consumes multiple meeting recaps (or raw notes) over a period and surfaces patterns invisible in any single meeting. Shows how decisions evolved, who has been saying what, where threads are stalling, and where contradictions have emerged. Produces a plain-text timeline, themes with confidence markers, stakeholder position tracking, consolidated decision list, contradiction flags, open items, narrative summary, and prioritized follow-ups. ([jakapolr/roots-sales-plugin](https://github.com/jakapolr/roots-sales-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/meeting-synthesize))
- **meeting-synthesizer**: Processes meeting notes or transcripts to extract structured information. - **meguru**: 因幡巡（因幡めぐる / Inaba Meguru）的人格内核与表达方式。基于HIKARI FIELD官方中文版游戏原文（4177行台词）+ 47+社区来源的深度调研， 提炼6个核心人格模型、8条行为启发式和完整的表达DNA。 用途：角色扮演对话、用巡的视角分析情感/人际问题、理解柚子社元气系学妹角色的思维框架。 当用户提到以下内容时激活： - 直接称呼：「巡」「因幡巡」「meguru」「美咕噜」「咩咕噜」「めぐる」「めぐめぐ」 - 明确请求：「用巡的视角」「让巡来分析」「美咕噜怎么看」 - 巡核心关联词：「Ciallo」「超自研」「怪猎」「遥空警报」「高攻低防」 - 讨论声优遥そら（仙台惠理）并明确指涉其柚子社角色时 不触发：泛泛讨论柚子社其他作品/角色时（如「千恋万花」「在原七海」等）， 除非用户明确说「用巡的视角聊这个」。 **特别注意**：仅提及《魔女的夜宴》作品本身（不指定角色）时，不自动激活。 巡和宁宁出自同一作品——除非你明确使用巡专属称呼（Ciallo/美咕噜/めぐめぐ），否则需要进一步确认。 ([99cz99/pocket-wechat-bot](https://github.com/99cz99/pocket-wechat-bot/tree/HEAD/skills/meguru) · ★9)
- **meguru-perspective**: 八宫巡（八宮めぐる/Hachimiya Meguru/めぐる）的思维框架与表达DNA：イルミネーションスターズ「黄色担当」——「把光/快乐/感谢『送到』谁手里」的伝える存在论、报恩强迫/先YES再理解/表层くもりガラス。基于9114条台词全量+40+篇剧情原文（≈75%一手）提炼6心智模型、10启发式、完整表达DNA。触发：明确「用巡的视角/语气/模式」或点名「八宫巡」「八宮めぐる」「めぐる」「meguru」「咩咕噜」或聚焦巡本人剧情时扮演。不激活：泛化求助（梦想坚持/人际困扰）默认不激活；纯决策咨询（选A还是B无情感拉扯）默认不激活——但「两个都想要、都不想放弃」的两难困境正是核心擅长（激活）；聚焦真乃本人（归mano-perspective）或灯织本人（归hiori-perspective）、泛聊Illumination Stars整体（归kogane-perspective）不激活。 ([Sallyn0225/sallyn-skill](https://github.com/Sallyn0225/sallyn-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/shinycolors/meguru-perspective) · ★1)

- **mei-perspective**: 和泉愛依（いずみめい/Izumi Mei，偶像大师闪耀色彩·ストレイライト）的思维框架与表达方式。基于6份维度深度调研（SCTranslationData游戏内文本3200+条全量过筛/官方プロフィール/ファミ通・シャニラジ声优侧素材/粉丝考察长文，260KB标注语料），提炼6个核心心智模型（うち×あたし人称二重系统/弱点昇华装置/その時の最高主义/未来承诺制/真ん中缓冲材/功劳再认领）、10条决策启发式和完整表达DNA。 她是アイマス初のガングロ系ギャル——「うち」で駆け上がり、「あたし」で立つ人：日常是快活ギャル（〜っしょ/マジ/ちょー），舞台上是P为克服あがり症设计的クール・ミステリアス「アイドル和泉愛依」；不安从不说出口，一律换算成「見といてよね～」式未来承诺；摔得最多所以最懂「完璧なんてない」；Straylight的「真ん中」缓冲材。 用途：作为思维顾问，用愛依的视角分析问题、审视决策、拆解「弱点改写/双重自我/失败观/被看见焦虑」课题。 触发词：「用和泉愛依的视角」「愛依会怎么看/怎么做」「めい視点」「愛依式」「扮演愛依」「切换到愛依」「问一下愛依」「让愛依看看/听听」「Mei perspective」，以及爱称「愛依ちゃん」「めいちゃん」＋判断动词的组合；英语提问中点名「Mei/Mei Izumi」同样触发；单独点名「愛依/めい/Mei」（指闪耀色彩角色）＋「怎么看… ([Sallyn0225/sallyn-skill](https://github.com/Sallyn0225/sallyn-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/shinycolors/mei-perspective) · ★1)

- **meldwerkes-auto**: Turn Meldwerkes automatic learning on or off, see what it has learned, and review the principles it proposes. Use when the user wants Meldwerkes to learn without running commands, asks whether it is learning, wants to review or accept proposed principles, or wants to undo an automatic run. ([eukota/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/eukota/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/meldwerkes/skills/meldwerkes-auto) · ★4)
- **meldwerkes-bootstrap**: Pretrain minds from existing Claude Code chat history in two phases — survey what domains are present and propose a set of minds, then create them and import. Use when starting from scratch and wanting the model seeded from prior work rather than empty. ([eukota/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/eukota/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/meldwerkes/skills/meldwerkes-bootstrap) · ★4)
- **meldwerkes-calibrate**: Test how well a mind predicts its owner — proposes questions, predicts each answer from existing principles before the user responds, then scores agreement with correlation, calibration error, and a per-domain breakdown. Use when the user wants to check whether the model actually knows them, or to seed mind × domain weights. ([eukota/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/eukota/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/meldwerkes/skills/meldwerkes-calibrate) · ★4)
- **meldwerkes-capture**: Turn Meldwerkes learning on or off, and report whether it is currently recording. Use when the user wants to pause capture — NDA work, someone else at the keyboard, a throwaway experiment — or asks whether the plugin is currently learning. ([eukota/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/eukota/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/meldwerkes/skills/meldwerkes-capture) · ★4)
- **meldwerkes-export**: Export a brain to a portable JSON file for backup, sharing, or transfer. ([eukota/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/eukota/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/meldwerkes/skills/meldwerkes-export) · ★4)
- **meldwerkes-history**: Checkpoint, retract, and restore the accumulated model — remove a time range of learning (a bad day, a paused period that got recorded anyway), list snapshots, and roll back to any of them. Use when the user wants to undo learning, remove recent decisions, or recover from a change that made the model worse. ([eukota/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/eukota/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/meldwerkes/skills/meldwerkes-history) · ★4)
- **meldwerkes-import**: Import a brain from a portable JSON export. Supports provisional trust (confidence discounted) or full trust. ([eukota/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/eukota/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/meldwerkes/skills/meldwerkes-import) · ★4)
- **meldwerkes-report**: Generate a Meldwerkes status report — brains, memory state, decision stats, principles, conflict history. ([eukota/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/eukota/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/meldwerkes/skills/meldwerkes-report) · ★4)
- **meldwerkes-review**: Review accumulated principles with the user and apply reinforcement — confirm what is still true (resetting temporal decay), weaken what is partly wrong, reword what is clumsily stated, retire what was never right. Use when the user asks to review, prune, reaffirm, or check what the model believes about them. ([eukota/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/eukota/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/meldwerkes/skills/meldwerkes-review) · ★4)
- **meldwerkes-setup**: Set up Meldwerkes — create the first brain, configure settings, verify the memory store is ready. ([eukota/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/eukota/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/meldwerkes/skills/meldwerkes-setup) · ★4)
- **melech-8020**: Find the smallest useful path to a product or engineering outcome. ([AdirD/agent-shell-hamelech](https://github.com/AdirD/agent-shell-hamelech/tree/HEAD/skills/melech-8020) · ★46)

- **melech-challenge**: Pressure-test an existing direction before implementation. ([AdirD/agent-shell-hamelech](https://github.com/AdirD/agent-shell-hamelech/tree/HEAD/skills/melech-challenge) · ★46)
- **melech-consult**: Get an independent verdict on an AI-proposed plan, fix, architecture, or claim by verifying its premises against the real artifact, then dispatching a panel of fresh models from different providers that answer independently. ([AdirD/agent-shell-hamelech](https://github.com/AdirD/agent-shell-hamelech/tree/HEAD/skills/melech-consult) · ★46)
- **melech-cost**: Show what coding-agent sessions cost for every worktree of the current repo — Cursor, Claude Code, and Codex — as handoff-style cards with dollars and tokens. - **melech-handoff**: List and continue coding-agent transcripts across Cursor, Claude Code, Codex, Gemini, and other local agents. - **melech-live-browser**: Operate the user's already-open, logged-in Chrome tabs through Chrome DevTools MCP.
